winner

def · Basic.lean:36 · GPDYS.winner
The weighted (discrete L²) inner product ⟪u, v⟫_h = ∑ᵢ wᵢ uᵢ vᵢ.
def winner (w : ι → ℝ) (u v : ι → ℝ) : ℝ := ∑ i, w i * (u i * v i)

Setting

structure · Basic.lean:72 · GPDYS.Setting
The data of the discrete ground state problem under Assumption 2.1, recorded through the fixed-mesh properties of Lemma 2.2. The structure assumes these properties; it does not construct a finite element mesh or express uniformity of CS across a family of meshes: * w are the quadrature weights (the lumped mass matrix is M = diag w), all positive; * S is the stiffness matrix: symmetric, positive semidefinite, with nonpositive off-diagonal entries, and such that (M + γ S)⁻¹ has strictly positive entries for every γ > 0 (Lemma 2.2 (ii), a consequence of M + γ S being an irreducible Stieltjes matrix); * V ≥ 0 is the potential at the nodes and β > 0 the coupling constant; * CS is the constant of the discrete Sobolev inequality ‖v‖₆ ≤ C_S ‖v‖_{1,h} (Lemma 2.2 (iii)), where ‖v‖²_{1,h} = ⟪v, -Δ_h v⟫_h + ‖v‖₂² = vᵀ S v + ‖v‖₂².
structure Setting (ι : Type*) [Fintype ι] [DecidableEq ι] where
  /-- quadrature weights -/
  w : ι → ℝ
  w_pos : ∀ i, 0 < w i
  /-- stiffness matrix -/
  S : Matrix ι ι ℝ
  S_symm : S.IsSymm
  S_psd : ∀ v : ι → ℝ, 0 ≤ v ⬝ᵥ (S *ᵥ v)
  S_offdiag : ∀ i j, i ≠ j → S i j ≤ 0
  /-- `(M + γ S)⁻¹` has strictly positive entries for every `γ > 0` -/
  resolvent_pos : ∀ γ : ℝ, 0 < γ → ∀ i j, 0 < (Matrix.diagonal w + γ • S)⁻¹ i j
  /-- potential -/
  V : ι → ℝ
  V_nonneg : ∀ i, 0 ≤ V i
  /-- coupling constant -/
  β : ℝ
  β_pos : 0 < β
  /-- Sobolev constant -/
  CS : ℝ
  sobolev : ∀ v : ι → ℝ, wnorm6 w v ≤ CS * Real.sqrt (v ⬝ᵥ (S *ᵥ v) + wnorm2sq w v)
Elaborated type
GPDYS.Setting :
(ι : Type u_2) → [Fintype ι] → [DecidableEq ι] → Type u_2

def · Basic.lean:164 · GPDYS.Setting.xi
The intermediate vector 𝐱 = G_X⁻¹ (2u - z - γ n(u)) of (DYS I).
def xi (γ : ℝ) (z : ι → ℝ) : ι → ℝ :=
  P.resolvent γ ((2 : ℝ) • P.normalize z - z - γ • P.nonlin (P.normalize z))

U0

def · Basic.lean:200 · GPDYS.Setting.U0
U₀ = min{1/w_min, P₀/w_min^{1/3}, 2 √(E₀/(β w_min))}, (3.10).
def U0 (u0 : ι → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  min (1 / P.wmin) (min (P.P0 u0 / P.wmin ^ ((1 : ℝ) / 3))
    (2 * Real.sqrt (P.E0 u0 / (P.β * P.wmin))))
Elaborated type

{ι : Type u_1} → [inst : Fintype ι] → [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] → GPDYS.Setting ι → (ι → ℝ) → ℝ

L0

def · Basic.lean:217 · GPDYS.Setting.L0
L₀ = ‖V‖_∞ + min{3 β U₀, C₀ + 2 max{C₀ - 1/4, 0}²}, (3.12).
def L0 (u0 : ι → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  P.Vinf + min (3 * P.β * P.U0 u0) (P.C0 u0 + 2 * (max (P.C0 u0 - 1 / 4) 0) ^ 2)
Elaborated type
GPDYS.Setting.L0 :
{ι : Type u_1} → [inst : Fintype ι] → [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] → GPDYS.Setting ι → (ι → ℝ) → ℝ

γmax

def · Basic.lean:222 · GPDYS.Setting.γmax
The step-size threshold γ_max = min{1, 1/(128 D), 1/(8 L₀), 2/(B₁ + √(B₁² + 12 D Λ_d))}, (3.14).
def γmax (u0 : ι → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  min 1 (min (1 / (128 * P.D u0)) (min (1 / (8 * P.L0 u0))
    (2 / (P.B1 u0 + Real.sqrt ((P.B1 u0) ^ 2 + 12 * P.D u0 * P.Λd)))))
Elaborated type
GPDYS.Setting.γmax :
{ι : Type u_1} → [inst : Fintype ι] → [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] → GPDYS.Setting ι → (ι → ℝ) → ℝ

Conditions

def · Basic.lean:228 · GPDYS.Setting.Conditions
The four step-size conditions (C1)–(C4) of Lemma 3.1, (3.15). Positivity of γ is supplied separately in the theorems using these conditions.
def Conditions (u0 : ι → ℝ) (γ : ℝ) : Prop :=
  γ ≤ 1 ∧ γ * P.D u0 ≤ 1 / 128 ∧ γ * P.L0 u0 ≤ 1 / 8 ∧
    γ * P.B1 u0 + 3 * γ ^ 2 * P.D u0 * P.Λd ≤ 1
Elaborated type
GPDYS.Setting.Conditions :
{ι : Type u_1} → [inst : Fintype ι] → [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] → GPDYS.Setting ι → (ι → ℝ) → ℝ → Prop

sq_le_of_psd

theorem · Lemmas.lean:21 · GPDYS.sq_le_of_psd
Cauchy–Schwarz for a symmetric positive semidefinite bilinear form on a real vector space.
GPDYS.sq_le_of_psd :
∀ {E : Type u_1} [inst : AddCommGroup E] [inst_1 : _root_.Module ℝ E] (B : E → E → ℝ),
  (∀ (u v w : E), B (u + v) w = B u w + B v w) →
    (∀ (c : ℝ) (u v : E), B (c • u) v = c * B u v) →
      (∀ (u v : E), B u v = B v u) → (∀ (v : E), 0 ≤ B v v) → ∀ (u v : E), B u v ^ 2 ≤ B u u * B v v

nrm2sq_GX

theorem · Resolvent.lean:130 · GPDYS.Setting.nrm2sq_GX
‖y + γ(-Δ_h y)‖₂² = ‖y‖₂² + 2γ ⟪y,-Δ_h y⟫_h + γ² ‖-Δ_h y‖₂².
GPDYS.Setting.nrm2sq_GX :

  0 ≤ γ → ∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.nrm2sq v - P.nrmXsq γ v ≤ γ * P.kin v

‖v‖_X² ≤ ‖v‖_𝒢².
GPDYS.Setting.nrmXsq_le_nrmGsq :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {γ : ℝ},
  0 ≤ γ → ∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.nrmXsq γ v ≤ P.nrmGsq γ v

nrm2sq_contraction

theorem · Resolvent.lean:282 · GPDYS.Setting.nrm2sq_contraction
For -1 ≤ a ≤ 0, the operator a I + (1 - a) G_X⁻¹ is a contraction in ‖·‖₂.
GPDYS.Setting.nrm2sq_contraction :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {γ a : ℝ},
  0 ≤ γ → -1 ≤ a → a ≤ 0 → ∀ (u : ι → ℝ), P.nrm2sq (a • u + (1 - a) • P.resolvent γ u) ≤ P.nrm2sq u

nrm2sq_negLap_le

theorem · Resolvent.lean:302 · GPDYS.Setting.nrm2sq_negLap_le
‖-Δ_h y‖₂² ≤ μ ⟪y,-Δ_h y⟫_h if ⟪·,-Δ_h ·⟫_h ≤ μ ‖·‖₂².
GPDYS.Setting.nrm2sq_negLap_le :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {μ : ℝ},
  0 ≤ μ → (∀ (x : ι → ℝ), P.kin x ≤ μ * P.nrm2sq x) → ∀ (y : ι → ℝ), P.nrm2sq (P.negLap y) ≤ μ * P.kin y

nrm2sq_le_mul_nrmXsq

theorem · Resolvent.lean:321 · GPDYS.Setting.nrm2sq_le_mul_nrmXsq
‖v‖₂² ≤ (1 + γ μ) ‖v‖_X² if ⟪·,-Δ_h ·⟫_h ≤ μ ‖·‖₂², (5.3).
GPDYS.Setting.nrm2sq_le_mul_nrmXsq :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {γ μ : ℝ},
  0 ≤ γ → 0 ≤ μ → (∀ (x : ι → ℝ), P.kin x ≤ μ * P.nrm2sq x) → ∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.nrm2sq v ≤ (1 + γ * μ) * P.nrmXsq γ v

GPDYS.holder_cubes :

GPDYS/SobolevConst.lean — a Sobolev constant exists on every fixed nonempty mesh

exists_sobolev_const

theorem · SobolevConst.lean:23 · GPDYS.exists_sobolev_const
On a fixed nonempty mesh a Sobolev constant exists for positive weights w and positive semidefinite S. The proof chooses (∑ᵢ wᵢ)^{1/6} / √(minᵢ wᵢ).
GPDYS.exists_sobolev_const :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [Nonempty ι] (w : ι → ℝ),
  (∀ (i : ι), 0 < w i) →
    ∀ (S : Matrix ι ι ℝ),
      (∀ (v : ι → ℝ), 0 ≤ v ⬝ᵥ S.mulVec v) →
        ∃ CS, 0 < CS ∧ ∀ (v : ι → ℝ), GPDYS.wnorm6 w v ≤ CS * √(v ⬝ᵥ S.mulVec v + GPDYS.wnorm2sq w v)

C4_identity

theorem · Constants.lean:204 · GPDYS.Setting.C4_identity
The key algebraic identity behind (C4): with s = √(B₁² + 12 D Λ_d), 4 (γ B₁ + 3 γ² D Λ_d - 1) = (γ (B₁ + s) - 2) (γ (s - B₁) + 2).
GPDYS.Setting.C4_identity :
∀ {B D Λ γ s : ℝ},
  s ^ 2 = B ^ 2 + 12 * D * Λ → 4 * (γ * B + 3 * γ ^ 2 * D * Λ - 1) = (γ * (B + s) - 2) * (γ * (s - B) + 2)

theorem · Constants.lean:275 · GPDYS.Setting.lyap_init_le_E0
Lemma 3.1, last assertion: 𝓛(z⁰) ≤ E₀ for z⁰ = u⁰ ∈ 𝓜. The proof uses 𝓛(z⁰) = E_h(u⁰) + γ λ(u⁰)².

GPDYS/Step.lean — Section 4.1: Lemma 4.1 and Lemma 4.2

delta

def · Step.lean:27 · GPDYS.Setting.delta
The change of direction δ = u⁺ - u of one step.
def delta (γ : ℝ) (z : ι → ℝ) : ι → ℝ := P.normalize (P.step γ z) - P.normalize z
Elaborated type
GPDYS.Setting.delta :
{ι : Type u_1} → [inst : Fintype ι] → [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] → GPDYS.Setting ι → ℝ → (ι → ℝ) → ι → ℝ

taylorT

def · Step.lean:31 · GPDYS.Setting.taylorT
The Taylor remainder 𝒯(u, d) = H(u + d) - H(u) - ⟪n(u), d⟫_h of the explicit part of the energy, (4.2).
def taylorT (u d : ι → ℝ) : ℝ := P.H (u + d) - P.H u - P.inner (P.nonlin u) d
Elaborated type
GPDYS.Setting.taylorT :
{ι : Type u_1} → [inst : Fintype ι] → [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] → GPDYS.Setting ι → (ι → ℝ) → (ι → ℝ) → ℝ

inner_sub_self_eq

theorem · Step.lean:36 · GPDYS.Setting.inner_sub_self_eq
⟪u, u⁺ - u⟫_h = -½ ‖u⁺ - u‖₂² for unit vectors u, u⁺.
GPDYS.Setting.inner_sub_self_eq :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {u up : ι → ℝ},
  P.nrm2sq u = 1 → P.nrm2sq up = 1 → P.inner u (up - u) = -(1 / 2) * P.nrm2sq (up - u)

nrm2sq_sub_eq

theorem · Step.lean:41 · GPDYS.Setting.nrm2sq_sub_eq
‖u⁺ - u‖₂² = 2 (1 - ⟪u, u⁺⟫_h) for unit vectors u, u⁺.
GPDYS.Setting.nrm2sq_sub_eq :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {u up : ι → ℝ},
  P.nrm2sq u = 1 → P.nrm2sq up = 1 → P.nrm2sq (up - u) = 2 * (1 - P.inner u up)

nrm2sq_delta_eq

theorem · Step.lean:46 · GPDYS.Setting.nrm2sq_delta_eq
‖δ‖₂² = 2 (1 - ⟪u, z⁺⟫_h / ‖z⁺‖₂), the second form in (4.1).
GPDYS.Setting.nrm2sq_delta_eq :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {γ : ℝ} {z : ι → ℝ},
  z ≠ 0 → P.step γ z ≠ 0 → P.nrm2sq (P.delta γ z) = 2 * (1 - P.inner (P.normalize z) (P.step γ z) / P.nrm2 (P.step γ z))

taylorT_eq

theorem · Step.lean:57 · GPDYS.Setting.taylorT_eq
The explicit polynomial form of the remainder: 𝒯(u, d) = ½ ∑ᵢ wᵢ Vᵢ dᵢ² + (β/2) ∑ᵢ wᵢ dᵢ² (3uᵢ² + 2uᵢdᵢ + ½dᵢ²).
GPDYS.Setting.taylorT_eq :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) (u d : ι → ℝ),
  P.taylorT u d =
    1 / 2 * ∑ i, P.w i * (P.V i * d i ^ 2) +
      P.β / 2 * ∑ i, P.w i * (d i ^ 2 * (3 * u i ^ 2 + 2 * u i * d i + d i ^ 2 / 2))

taylorE

theorem · Step.lean:67 · GPDYS.Setting.taylorE
The Taylor expansion (4.3): E_h(u + d) - E_h(u) = ⟪∇E_h(u), d⟫_h + ½ ⟪d, -Δ_h d⟫_h + 𝒯(u, d).
GPDYS.Setting.taylorE :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) (u d : ι → ℝ),
  P.energy (u + d) - P.energy u = P.inner (P.gradE u) d + 1 / 2 * P.kin d + P.taylorT u d

GPDYS.Setting.GX_eq_nrm2_smul :

GX_step_aux

theorem · Step.lean:84 · GPDYS.Setting.GX_step_aux
The first identity of (4.4) with z = c • u substituted: for any scalar c and vector u, G_X (c u + G_X⁻¹(2u - c u - γ n(u)) - u) = u - γ n(u) - γ (1 - c) (-Δ_h u).
GPDYS.Setting.GX_step_aux :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {γ : ℝ},
  0 ≤ γ →
    ∀ (c : ℝ) (u : ι → ℝ),
      P.GX γ (c • u + P.resolvent γ (2 • u - c • u - γ • P.nonlin u) - u) =
        u - γ • P.nonlin u - (γ * (1 - c)) • P.negLap u

theorem · Step.lean:98 · GPDYS.Setting.GX_step

theorem · Step.lean:138 · GPDYS.Setting.radial_identity

energy_identity

theorem · Step.lean:167 · GPDYS.Setting.energy_identity
Lemma 4.2, (4.9): the energy identity γ(E_h(u) - E_h(u⁺)) = ((‖z⁺‖₂+1)/2)‖δ‖₂² + γ(‖z⁺‖₂ - ½)⟪δ,-Δ_h δ⟫_h + γ(‖z⁺‖₂ - ‖z‖₂)⟪-Δ_h u, δ⟫_h - γ 𝒯(u, δ).
GPDYS.Setting.energy_identity :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {γ : ℝ},
  0 ≤ γ →
    ∀ {z : ι → ℝ},
      z ≠ 0 →
        P.step γ z ≠ 0 →
          γ * (P.energy (P.normalize z) - P.energy (P.normalize (P.step γ z))) =
            (P.nrm2 (P.step γ z) + 1) / 2 * P.nrm2sq (P.delta γ z) +
                  γ * (P.nrm2 (P.step γ z) - 1 / 2) * P.kin (P.delta γ z) +
                γ * (P.nrm2 (P.step γ z) - P.nrm2 z) * P.normalize z ⬝ᵥ P.S.mulVec (P.delta γ z) -

theorem · Step.lean:195 · GPDYS.Setting.residual_identity

sobolev_quadratic_bound_aux

theorem · Nonlinear.lean:31 · GPDYS.Setting.sobolev_quadratic_bound_aux
The elementary inequality behind (4.13), for 0 ≤ x ≤ y and an arbitrary real C₀ (no sign condition on C₀ is needed): C₀ x y ≤ (C₀ + 2 max{C₀ - 1/4, 0}²) x² + (y² - x²)/8.
GPDYS.Setting.sobolev_quadratic_bound_aux :

theorem · Nonlinear.lean:52 · GPDYS.Setting.taylorT_le_pointwise

GPDYS.Setting.taylorT_le_pointwise :

taylorT_le_sobolev

theorem · Nonlinear.lean:98 · GPDYS.Setting.taylorT_le_sobolev
The Taylor-remainder consequence of the first Jacobian bound in (4.13): 𝒯(u, δ) ≤ ½ (‖V‖_∞ ‖δ‖₂² + C₀ ‖δ‖₂ ‖δ‖_{1,h}), with ‖δ‖_{1,h} = √(⟪δ,-Δ_h δ⟫_h + ‖δ‖₂²).
GPDYS.Setting.taylorT_le_sobolev :

  P.nrm2sq u = 1 →
    P.nrm2sq up = 1 →
      P.energy u ≤ P.E0 u0 →
        P.energy up ≤ P.E0 u0 →
          ∀ [Nonempty ι],
            P.taylorT u (up - u) ≤
              1 / 2 * (P.Vinf * P.nrm2sq (up - u) + P.C0 u0 * P.nrm2 (up - u) * √(P.kin (up - u) + P.nrm2sq (up - u)))

abs_inner_nonlin_sub_le

theorem · Nonlinear.lean:263 · GPDYS.Setting.abs_inner_nonlin_sub_le
Lemma 4.3, second inequality (4.11): |⟪n(u⁺) - n(u), u⁺⟫_h| ≤ 3 B₀ ‖δ‖₂.
GPDYS.Setting.abs_inner_nonlin_sub_le :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {u0 u up : ι → ℝ},

    P.nrm2sq up = 1 →
      P.energy u ≤ P.E0 u0 →
        P.energy up ≤ P.E0 u0 → ∀ [Nonempty ι], |P.inner (P.nonlin up - P.nonlin u) up| ≤ 3 * P.B0 u0 * P.nrm2 (up - u)

sobolev_quadratic_bound

theorem · Nonlinear.lean:323 · GPDYS.Setting.sobolev_quadratic_bound
The elementary inequality behind (4.13): for 0 ≤ x ≤ y, C₀ x y ≤ (C₀ + 2 max{C₀ - 1/4, 0}²) x² + (y² - x²)/8.
GPDYS.Setting.sobolev_quadratic_bound :
∀ (C0 : ℝ),
  0 ≤ C0 → ∀ {x y : ℝ}, 0 ≤ x → x ≤ y → C0 * x * y ≤ (C0 + 2 * max (C0 - 1 / 4) 0 ^ 2) * x ^ 2 + (y ^ 2 - x ^ 2) / 8

step_eq

theorem · Positivity.lean:69 · GPDYS.Setting.step_eq
(4.16): z⁺ = (‖z‖₂ - 1) u + (2 - ‖z‖₂) G_X⁻¹ u - γ G_X⁻¹ n(u).
GPDYS.Setting.step_eq :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {γ : ℝ} {z : ι → ℝ},
  z ≠ 0 →
    P.step γ z =
      (P.nrm2 z - 1) • P.normalize z + (2 - P.nrm2 z) • P.resolvent γ (P.normalize z) -
        γ • P.resolvent γ (P.nonlin (P.normalize z))

                    |P.nrm2 (P.step γ z) - P.nrm2 z| ≤ |P.R γ z| + 8 / 3 * √(γ * P.D u0) * √(P.nrmGsq γ (P.delta γ z))

energy_step_le_of_small

theorem · Descent.lean:248 · GPDYS.Setting.energy_step_le_of_small
Auxiliary used here for Lemma 4.7: if γ(28D + 2C₁) ≤ 1 then E_h(u⁺) ≤ E_h(u) + 2D R².
GPDYS.Setting.energy_step_le_of_small :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι] {u0 : ι → ℝ} {γ : ℝ},
  0 < γ →
    γ * P.D u0 ≤ 1 / 128 →
      γ * P.B1 u0 + 3 * γ ^ 2 * P.D u0 * P.Λd ≤ 1 →
        ∀ {z : ι → ℝ},
          z ≠ 0 →
            (∀ (i : ι), 0 < P.normalize z i) →
              P.energy (P.normalize z) ≤ P.E0 u0 →
                1 - 3 * γ * P.D u0 ≤ P.nrm2 z →
                  P.nrm2 z ≤ 1 →
                    γ * (28 * P.D u0 + 2 * P.C1) ≤ 1 →
                      P.energy (P.normalize (P.step γ z)) ≤ P.energy (P.normalize z) + 2 * P.D u0 * P.R γ z ^ 2

energy_drop

theorem · Descent.lean:350 · GPDYS.Setting.energy_drop
(4.22): the energy drop γ(E_h(u) - E_h(u⁺)) ≥ (7/16 - 10γD)‖δ‖_𝒢² - (1/5)R².
GPDYS.Setting.energy_drop :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι] {u0 : ι → ℝ} {γ : ℝ},
  0 < γ →
    γ * P.D u0 ≤ 1 / 128 →
      γ * P.L0 u0 ≤ 1 / 8 →
        γ * P.B1 u0 + 3 * γ ^ 2 * P.D u0 * P.Λd ≤ 1 →
          ∀ {z : ι → ℝ},
            z ≠ 0 →
              (∀ (i : ι), 0 < P.normalize z i) →
                P.energy (P.normalize z) ≤ P.E0 u0 →
                  1 - 3 * γ * P.D u0 ≤ P.nrm2 z →
                    P.nrm2 z ≤ 1 →
                      P.energy (P.normalize (P.step γ z)) ≤ P.E0 u0 →
                        γ * (P.energy (P.normalize z) - P.energy (P.normalize (P.step γ z))) ≥
                          (7 / 16 - 10 * γ * P.D u0) * P.nrmGsq γ (P.delta γ z) - 1 / 5 * P.R γ z ^ 2

GPDYS.Setting.residual_sq_le :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι] {u0 : ι → ℝ} {γ : ℝ},

descent_equality_case

theorem · Descent.lean:582 · GPDYS.Setting.descent_equality_case
Lemma 4.5, equality case: if δ = 0 and R = 0 then g(u) = 0 and z⁺ = z. (For γ = 0 one has z⁺ = u and δ = 0 for every z, so γ > 0 is needed for g(u) = 0.)
GPDYS.Setting.descent_equality_case :

  0 < γ →
    ∀ {z : ι → ℝ}, z ≠ 0 → P.step γ z ≠ 0 → P.delta γ z = 0 → P.R γ z = 0 → P.g (P.normalize z) = 0 ∧ P.step γ z = z

nrmGsq_mul_nrm2_sub

theorem · Descent.lean:624 · GPDYS.Setting.nrmGsq_mul_nrm2_sub
The radial identity in the form ‖u‖_𝒢² (‖z⁺‖₂ - ‖z‖₂) = -R - ‖z⁺‖₂ ⟪u, G_X δ⟫_h.
GPDYS.Setting.nrmGsq_mul_nrm2_sub :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι] {u0 : ι → ℝ} {γ : ℝ},
  0 < γ →
    γ * P.D u0 ≤ 1 / 128 →
      γ * P.B1 u0 + 3 * γ ^ 2 * P.D u0 * P.Λd ≤ 1 →
        ∀ {z : ι → ℝ},
          z ≠ 0 →
            (∀ (i : ι), 0 < P.normalize z i) →
              P.energy (P.normalize z) ≤ P.E0 u0 →
                1 - 3 * γ * P.D u0 ≤ P.nrm2 z →
                  P.nrm2 z ≤ 1 →
                    P.nrmGsq γ (P.normalize z) * (P.nrm2 (P.step γ z) - P.nrm2 z) =
                      -P.R γ z - P.nrm2 (P.step γ z) * P.inner (P.normalize z) (P.GX γ (P.delta γ z))

step_sub_eq_perp

theorem · Descent.lean:638 · GPDYS.Setting.step_sub_eq_perp
The decomposition z⁺ - z = ‖z⁺‖₂ δ⊥ - (R/‖u‖_𝒢²) u, where δ⊥ = δ - (⟪u, G_X δ⟫_h/‖u‖_𝒢²) u is the 𝒢-orthogonal part of δ.
GPDYS.Setting.step_sub_eq_perp :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι] {u0 : ι → ℝ} {γ : ℝ},
  0 < γ →
    γ * P.D u0 ≤ 1 / 128 →
      γ * P.B1 u0 + 3 * γ ^ 2 * P.D u0 * P.Λd ≤ 1 →
        ∀ {z : ι → ℝ},
          z ≠ 0 →
            (∀ (i : ι), 0 < P.normalize z i) →
              P.energy (P.normalize z) ≤ P.E0 u0 →
                1 - 3 * γ * P.D u0 ≤ P.nrm2 z →
                  P.nrm2 z ≤ 1 →
                    P.step γ z - z =
                      P.nrm2 (P.step γ z) •
                          (P.delta γ z -
                            (P.inner (P.normalize z) (P.GX γ (P.delta γ z)) / P.nrmGsq γ (P.normalize z)) •
                              P.normalize z) -
                        (P.R γ z / P.nrmGsq γ (P.normalize z)) • P.normalize z

theorem · Barrier.lean:192 · GPDYS.Setting.zcont_hyp
Conditions used in Lemma 4.7: for θ ∈ (0,1], (C2)–(C4) hold for θγ, and 1 - 3θγD ≤ ‖z_θ‖₂ ≤ 1. Positivity and the energy bound are proved separately.
GPDYS.Setting.zcont_hyp :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι] {u0 : ι → ℝ},
  u0 ≠ 0 →
    ∀ {γ : ℝ},
      0 < γ →
        γ * P.D u0 ≤ 1 / 128 →

            γ * P.B1 u0 + 3 * γ ^ 2 * P.D u0 * P.Λd ≤ 1 →
              ∀ {z : ι → ℝ},

                  (∀ (i : ι), 0 < P.normalize z i) →

                        P.lyap γ z ≤ P.E0 u0 →
                          ∀ {θ : ℝ},
                            0 < θ →
                              θ ≤ 1 →
                                θ * γ * P.D u0 ≤ 1 / 128 ∧
                                  θ * γ * P.L0 u0 ≤ 1 / 8 ∧
                                    θ * γ * P.B1 u0 + 3 * (θ * γ) ^ 2 * P.D u0 * P.Λd ≤ 1 ∧

              ∀ {z : ι → ℝ},

                  (∀ (i : ι), 0 < P.normalize z i) →
                    1 - 3 * γ * P.D u0 ≤ P.nrm2 z →
                      P.nrm2 z ≤ 1 → P.lyap γ z ≤ P.E0 u0 → ContinuousOn (P.φcont γ z) (Set.Icc 0 1)

φcont_le_of_le

theorem · Barrier.lean:298 · GPDYS.Setting.φcont_le_of_le
If φ(θ) ≤ E₀ for some θ ∈ (0,1], then φ(θ) ≤ E_h(u) + θ R²/(4γ) - (5/(16 θγ)) ‖δ_θ‖_𝒢², by the conditional descent.
GPDYS.Setting.φcont_le_of_le :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι] {u0 : ι → ℝ},
  u0 ≠ 0 →
    ∀ {γ : ℝ},
      0 < γ →
        γ * P.D u0 ≤ 1 / 128 →
          γ * P.L0 u0 ≤ 1 / 8 →
            γ * P.B1 u0 + 3 * γ ^ 2 * P.D u0 * P.Λd ≤ 1 →
              ∀ {z : ι → ℝ},
                z ≠ 0 →
                  (∀ (i : ι), 0 < P.normalize z i) →
                    1 - 3 * γ * P.D u0 ≤ P.nrm2 z →
                      P.nrm2 z ≤ 1 →
                        P.lyap γ z ≤ P.E0 u0 →
                          ∀ {θ : ℝ},
                            0 < θ →
                              θ ≤ 1 →
                                P.φcont γ z θ ≤ P.E0 u0 →
                                  P.φcont γ z θ ≤
                                    P.energy (P.normalize z) + θ * P.R γ z ^ 2 / (4 * γ) -
                                      5 / (16 * (θ * γ)) * P.nrmGsq (θ * γ) (P.delta (θ * γ) (P.zcont z θ))

GPDYS.Setting.φcont_lt_of_le :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι] {u0 : ι → ℝ},

GPDYS.Setting.φcont_eq_of_R_g :

onestep_drop

theorem · OneStep.lean:60 · GPDYS.Setting.onestep_drop
Proposition 4.8, (4.31) first inequality: 𝓛(z) - 𝓛(z⁺) ≥ ¼ γ ‖g(u)‖_X² + R²/(2γ).
GPDYS.Setting.onestep_drop :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι] {u0 : ι → ℝ},
  u0 ≠ 0 →
    ∀ {γ : ℝ},
      0 < γ →
        γ ≤ P.γmax u0 →
          ∀ {z : ι → ℝ},
            z ≠ 0 →
              (∀ (i : ι), 0 < P.normalize z i) →
                1 - 3 * γ * P.D u0 ≤ P.nrm2 z →
                  P.nrm2 z ≤ 1 →
                    P.lyap γ z ≤ P.E0 u0 →
                      P.lyap γ z - P.lyap γ (P.step γ z) ≥
                        1 / 4 * γ * P.nrmXsq γ (P.g (P.normalize z)) + P.R γ z ^ 2 / (2 * γ)

GPDYS.Setting.onestep_drop' :

  u0 ≠ 0 →

          ∀ {z : ι → ℝ},

energy_ge_of_critical

theorem · GroundState.lean:188 · GPDYS.Setting.energy_ge_of_critical
The key inequality used here to prove Remark 2.3: for a positive critical point u ∈ 𝓜 and any nonnegative v ∈ 𝓜, E_h(v) ≥ E_h(u) + (β/4) ∑ᵢ wᵢ (vᵢ² - uᵢ²)².
GPDYS.Setting.energy_ge_of_critical :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {u v : ι → ℝ},
  P.nrm2sq u = 1 →
    (∀ (i : ι), 0 < u i) →
      P.g u = 0 →
        P.nrm2sq v = 1 → (∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ v i) → P.energy u + P.β / 4 * ∑ i, P.w i * (v i ^ 2 - u i ^ 2) ^ 2 ≤ P.energy v

invariant

theorem · Main.lean:119 · GPDYS.Setting.invariant
Theorem 3.4, the invariant (5.1): for every k, u^k > 0, 1 - 3γD ≤ ‖z^k‖₂ ≤ 1 and 𝓛^k ≤ E₀.
GPDYS.Setting.invariant :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι] {u0 : ι → ℝ},
  (∀ (i : ι), 0 < u0 i) →
    P.nrm2 u0 = 1 →
      ∀ {γ : ℝ},
        0 < γ →
          γ ≤ P.γmax u0 →
            ∀ (k : ℕ),
              (∀ (i : ι), 0 < P.uiter γ u0 k i) ∧
                1 - 3 * γ * P.D u0 ≤ P.nrm2 (P.iter γ u0 k) ∧
                  P.nrm2 (P.iter γ u0 k) ≤ 1 ∧ P.lyap γ (P.iter γ u0 k) ≤ P.E0 u0

drop

theorem · Main.lean:162 · GPDYS.Setting.drop
Theorem 3.4, the drop (5.2): 𝓛^k - 𝓛^{k+1} ≥ ¼γ‖g(u^k)‖_X² + (R^k)²/(2γ).
GPDYS.Setting.drop :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι] {u0 : ι → ℝ},
  (∀ (i : ι), 0 < u0 i) →
    P.nrm2 u0 = 1 →
      ∀ {γ : ℝ},
        0 < γ →
          γ ≤ P.γmax u0 →
            ∀ (k : ℕ),
              P.lyap γ (P.iter γ u0 k) - P.lyap γ (P.iter γ u0 (k + 1)) ≥
                1 / 4 * γ * P.nrmXsq γ (P.g (P.uiter γ u0 k)) + P.R γ (P.iter γ u0 k) ^ 2 / (2 * γ)

sum_drop_le

theorem · Main.lean:173 · GPDYS.Setting.sum_drop_le
Theorem 3.4: every finite partial sum ∑_{k < m} (¼γ‖g^k‖_X² + (R^k)²/(2γ)) is at most 𝓛⁰.
GPDYS.Setting.sum_drop_le :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι] {u0 : ι → ℝ},

    P.nrm2 u0 = 1 →
      ∀ {γ : ℝ},
        0 < γ →
          γ ≤ P.γmax u0 →
            ∀ (m : ℕ),
              ∑ k ∈ Finset.range m,
                  (1 / 4 * γ * P.nrmXsq γ (P.g (P.uiter γ u0 k)) + P.R γ (P.iter γ u0 k) ^ 2 / (2 * γ)) ≤
                P.lyap γ u0

main_theorem

theorem · Main.lean:292 · GPDYS.Setting.main_theorem
Theorem 3.4 (global convergence to the discrete ground state). Under Assumption 2.1 (through Lemma 2.2), for a positive normalized initial vector u⁰ and a step size 0 < γ ≤ γ_max, the iterates u^k = z^k/‖z^k‖₂ of (DYS I) are positive for every k, and converge to the discrete ground state u_GS, the unique positive global minimizer of E_h on 𝓜, which is also the unique nonnegative critical point of E_h|_𝓜; moreover E_h(u^k) → E_h(u_GS) = min_𝓜 E_h.
GPDYS.Setting.main_theorem :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι] {u0 : ι → ℝ},
  (∀ (i : ι), 0 < u0 i) →
    P.nrm2 u0 = 1 →
      ∀ {γ : ℝ},
        0 < γ →
          γ ≤ P.γmax u0 →
            (∀ (k : ℕ) (i : ι), 0 < P.uiter γ u0 k i) ∧
              ∃ uGS,
                (∀ (i : ι), 0 < uGS i) ∧
                  P.nrm2 uGS = 1 ∧
                    P.g uGS = 0 ∧
                      (∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.nrm2 v = 1 → P.energy uGS ≤ P.energy v) ∧
                        (∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.nrm2 v = 1 → (∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ v i) → P.energy v ≤ P.energy uGS → v = uGS) ∧
                          (∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.nrm2 v = 1 → (∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ v i) → P.g v = 0 → v = uGS) ∧
                            Filter.Tendsto (P.uiter γ u0) Filter.atTop (nhds uGS) ∧
                              Filter.Tendsto (fun k => P.energy (P.uiter γ u0 k)) Filter.atTop (nhds (P.energy uGS))

exists_groundState

theorem · Main.lean:338 · GPDYS.Setting.exists_groundState
Corollary (the discrete ground state, Remark 2.3). There is a positive u_GS ∈ 𝓜 which is a critical point of E_h|_𝓜, the global minimizer of E_h on 𝓜, and the unique nonnegative critical point. It is obtained from Theorem 3.4 as the limit of the iteration started from the normalized constant vector with the step size γ_max.
GPDYS.Setting.exists_groundState :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι],
  ∃ uGS,
    (∀ (i : ι), 0 < uGS i) ∧
      P.nrm2 uGS = 1 ∧
        P.g uGS = 0 ∧
          (∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.nrm2 v = 1 → P.energy uGS ≤ P.energy v) ∧
            ∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.nrm2 v = 1 → (∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ v i) → P.g v = 0 → v = uGS

def hess (p w : ι → ℝ) : ι → ℝ := P.negLap w + P.nonlin' p w - P.lam p • w

nu

def · Rate.lean:51 · GPDYS.Setting.nu
The infimum of ⟪w, ℋ w⟫_h over unit tangent vectors. For a positive critical point and 2 ≤ Fintype.card ι, exists_unit_tangent and bddBelow_nu_set justify the infimum used in the rate theorem. Attainment as a minimum is proved by nu_attained in Spectral.lean. The empty-set value is zero.
def nu (p : ι → ℝ) : ℝ :=
  sInf {x : ℝ | ∃ w : ι → ℝ, P.nrm2sq w = 1 ∧ P.inner p w = 0 ∧ x = P.hessForm p w}
Elaborated type
GPDYS.Setting.nu :

hessForm_eq

theorem · Rate.lean:56 · GPDYS.Setting.hessForm_eq
GPDYS.Setting.hessForm_eq :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) (p w : ι → ℝ),
  P.hessForm p w = P.kin w + P.pot w + 3 * P.β * ∑ i, P.w i * (p i ^ 2 * w i ^ 2) - P.lam p * P.nrm2sq w

rayleigh_of_critical

theorem · Rate.lean:71 · GPDYS.Setting.rayleigh_of_critical
The Rayleigh bound of a positive critical point (via the discrete Picone inequality): ⟪w, A_p w⟫_h ≥ λ(p) ‖w‖₂² for every w, where A_p = -Δ_h + V + β diag(p²).
GPDYS.Setting.rayleigh_of_critical :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {p : ι → ℝ},
  P.nrm2sq p = 1 →
    (∀ (i : ι), 0 < p i) →
      P.g p = 0 → ∀ (w : ι → ℝ), P.lam p * P.nrm2sq w ≤ P.kin w + P.pot w + P.β * ∑ i, P.w i * (p i ^ 2 * w i ^ 2)

energy_sub_eq

theorem · Rate.lean:248 · GPDYS.Setting.energy_sub_eq
Exact energy expansion used here to prove Theorem 5.1, for u, p ∈ 𝓜 with p critical and h = u - p: E_h(u) - E_h(p) = ½⟪h, ℋ h⟫_h + β ∑ wᵢ pᵢ hᵢ³ + (β/4) ∑ wᵢ hᵢ⁴.
GPDYS.Setting.energy_sub_eq :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {p u : ι → ℝ},
  P.nrm2sq p = 1 →
    P.g p = 0 →
      P.nrm2sq u = 1 →
        P.energy u - P.energy p =
          1 / 2 * P.hessForm p (u - p) + P.β * ∑ i, P.w i * (p i * (u i - p i) ^ 3) +
            P.β / 4 * ∑ i, P.w i * (u i - p i) ^ 4

rho

def · Rate.lean:274 · GPDYS.Setting.rho
The vector ρ = β(3 p h² + h³).
def rho (p h : ι → ℝ) : ι → ℝ := fun i => P.β * (3 * p i * h i ^ 2 + h i ^ 3)
Elaborated type
GPDYS.Setting.rho :
{ι : Type u_1} → [inst : Fintype ι] → [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] → GPDYS.Setting ι → (ι → ℝ) → (ι → ℝ) → ι → ℝ

theorem · Rate.lean:277 · GPDYS.Setting.gradE_eq_of_critical
∇E_h(u) = λ(p) u + ℋ h + ρ for h = u - p, p critical.
GPDYS.Setting.gradE_eq_of_critical :

  P.g p = 0 → P.gradE u = P.lam p • u + P.hess p (u - p) + P.rho p (u - p)

inner_g_sub_eq

theorem · Rate.lean:291 · GPDYS.Setting.inner_g_sub_eq
Exact expansion used here to prove Theorem 5.1: ⟪g(u), h⟫_h, using ⟪u, h⟫_h = ½‖h‖₂²: ⟪g(u), h⟫_h = ⟪h, ℋ h⟫_h + ⟪ρ, h⟫_h - ½‖h‖₂² ⟪ℋ h + ρ, u⟫_h.
GPDYS.Setting.inner_g_sub_eq :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {p u : ι → ℝ},
  P.nrm2sq p = 1 →
    P.g p = 0 →
      P.nrm2sq u = 1 →
        P.inner (P.g u) (u - p) =
          P.hessForm p (u - p) + P.inner (P.rho p (u - p)) (u - p) -
            1 / 2 * P.nrm2sq (u - p) * P.inner (P.hess p (u - p) + P.rho p (u - p)) u

hess_self

theorem · Rate.lean:319 · GPDYS.Setting.hess_self
ℋ p = 2β p³ for a critical point p.
GPDYS.Setting.hess_self :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {p : ι → ℝ},
  P.g p = 0 → P.hess p p = (2 * P.β) • fun i => p i ^ 3

hessForm_decomp

theorem · Rate.lean:327 · GPDYS.Setting.hessForm_decomp
Decomposition of the Hessian form along h = (h - c p) + c p.
GPDYS.Setting.hessForm_decomp :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) (p h : ι → ℝ) (c : ℝ),
  P.hessForm p h = P.hessForm p (h - c • p) + 2 * c * P.inner (h - c • p) (P.hess p p) + c ^ 2 * P.inner p (P.hess p p)

hessForm_sub_ge_aux

theorem · Rate.lean:339 · GPDYS.Setting.hessForm_sub_ge_aux
The elementary inequality behind hessForm_sub_ge.
GPDYS.Setting.hessForm_sub_ge_aux :
∀ {ν β N3 R HT X Y c : ℝ},
  c = -(1 / 2) * R ^ 2 →
    ν * (R ^ 2 - c ^ 2) ≤ HT →
      0 ≤ Y → |X| ≤ R * (2 * β * N3) → ν * R ^ 2 - ν / 4 * (R ^ 2) ^ 2 - 2 * β * N3 * R ^ 3 ≤ HT + 2 * c * X + c ^ 2 * Y

hessForm_sub_ge

theorem · Rate.lean:353 · GPDYS.Setting.hessForm_sub_ge
The tangential lower bound: ⟪h, ℋ h⟫_h ≥ ν‖h‖₂² - (ν/4)‖h‖₂⁴ - 2β‖p³‖₂ ‖h‖₂³ for h = u - p with u, p ∈ 𝓜.
GPDYS.Setting.hessForm_sub_ge :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {p u : ι → ℝ},
  P.nrm2sq p = 1 →
    (∀ (i : ι), 0 < p i) →
      P.g p = 0 →
        P.nrm2sq u = 1 →
          P.nu p * P.nrm2sq (u - p) - P.nu p / 4 * P.nrm2sq (u - p) ^ 2 -
              (2 * P.β * P.nrm2 fun i => p i ^ 3) * P.nrm2 (u - p) ^ 3 ≤
            P.hessForm p (u - p)

theorem · Rate.lean:667 · GPDYS.Setting.eventually_close
The iterates are eventually within any distance r > 0 of p.
GPDYS.Setting.eventually_close :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι],
  2 ≤ Fintype.card ι →
    ∀ {u0 : ι → ℝ},
      (∀ (i : ι), 0 < u0 i) →
        P.nrm2 u0 = 1 →
          ∀ {γ : ℝ},
            0 < γ →
              γ ≤ P.γmax u0 →
                ∀ {μ : ℝ},
                  0 ≤ μ →
                    (∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.kin v ≤ μ * P.nrm2sq v) →
                      ∀ {p : ι → ℝ},
                        P.nrm2sq p = 1 →
                          (∀ (i : ι), 0 < p i) →
                            P.g p = 0 →
                              Filter.Tendsto (P.uiter γ u0) Filter.atTop (nhds p) →
                                ∀ {r : ℝ}, 0 < r → ∃ K, ∀ (k : ℕ), K ≤ k → P.nrm2 (P.uiter γ u0 k - p) ≤ r

rate_step

theorem · Rate.lean:693 · GPDYS.Setting.rate_step
Theorem 5.1, first inequality of (5.6): there is K such that for k ≥ K, 𝓛^{k+1} - E_GS ≤ (1 - η)(𝓛^k - E_GS).
GPDYS.Setting.rate_step :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι],
  2 ≤ Fintype.card ι →
    ∀ {u0 : ι → ℝ},
      (∀ (i : ι), 0 < u0 i) →
        P.nrm2 u0 = 1 →
          ∀ {γ : ℝ},
            0 < γ →
              γ ≤ P.γmax u0 →
                ∀ {μ : ℝ},
                  0 ≤ μ →
                    (∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.kin v ≤ μ * P.nrm2sq v) →
                      ∀ {p : ι → ℝ},
                        P.nrm2sq p = 1 →
                          (∀ (i : ι), 0 < p i) →
                            P.g p = 0 →
                              Filter.Tendsto (P.uiter γ u0) Filter.atTop (nhds p) →
                                ∃ K,
                                  ∀ (k : ℕ),
                                    K ≤ k →
                                      P.lyap γ (P.iter γ u0 (k + 1)) - P.energy p ≤
                                        (1 - GPDYS.η γ μ (P.nu p)) * (P.lyap γ (P.iter γ u0 k) - P.energy p)

rate_theorem

theorem · Rate.lean:749 · GPDYS.Setting.rate_theorem
Theorem 5.1, conditional form: assume the section hypotheses, including convergence of the iterates to the positive normalized critical point p, and a Rayleigh bound μ ≥ 0. Then there is an index K such that for every k ≥ K, 𝓛^{k+1} - E_GS ≤ (1-η)(𝓛^k - E_GS) and E_h(u^k) - E_GS + (R^k)²/γ + (ν/4)‖u^k - u_GS‖₂² ≤ 2(1-η)^{k-K}(𝓛^K - E_GS).
GPDYS.Setting.rate_theorem :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι],
  2 ≤ Fintype.card ι →
    ∀ {u0 : ι → ℝ},
      (∀ (i : ι), 0 < u0 i) →
        P.nrm2 u0 = 1 →
          ∀ {γ : ℝ},
            0 < γ →
              γ ≤ P.γmax u0 →
                ∀ {μ : ℝ},
                  0 ≤ μ →
                    (∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.kin v ≤ μ * P.nrm2sq v) →
                      ∀ {p : ι → ℝ},
                        P.nrm2sq p = 1 →
                          (∀ (i : ι), 0 < p i) →
                            P.g p = 0 →
                              Filter.Tendsto (P.uiter γ u0) Filter.atTop (nhds p) →
                                ∃ K,
                                  ∀ (k : ℕ),
                                    K ≤ k →
                                      P.lyap γ (P.iter γ u0 (k + 1)) - P.energy p ≤
                                          (1 - GPDYS.η γ μ (P.nu p)) * (P.lyap γ (P.iter γ u0 k) - P.energy p) ∧
                                        P.energy (P.uiter γ u0 k) - P.energy p + P.R γ (P.iter γ u0 k) ^ 2 / γ +
                                            P.nu p / 4 * P.nrm2sq (P.uiter γ u0 k - p) ≤
                                          2 * (1 - GPDYS.η γ μ (P.nu p)) ^ (k - K) *
                                            (P.lyap γ (P.iter γ u0 K) - P.energy p)

rate_theorem'

theorem · Rate.lean:798 · GPDYS.Setting.rate_theorem'
Theorem 5.1, self-contained form: under the hypotheses of Theorem 3.4, N ≥ 2, and any Rayleigh bound μ for -Δ_h, the iterates converge to the discrete ground state u_GS, the constant ν satisfies ν ≥ 2β m_GS² > 0, 0 < η < 1, and the geometric decay (5.6) holds from some index K on.
GPDYS.Setting.rate_theorem' :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι],
  2 ≤ Fintype.card ι →
    ∀ {u0 : ι → ℝ},
      (∀ (i : ι), 0 < u0 i) →
        P.nrm2 u0 = 1 →
          ∀ {γ : ℝ},
            0 < γ →
              γ ≤ P.γmax u0 →
                ∀ {μ : ℝ},
                  0 ≤ μ →
                    (∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.kin v ≤ μ * P.nrm2sq v) →
                      ∃ uGS,
                        (∀ (i : ι), 0 < uGS i) ∧
                          P.nrm2 uGS = 1 ∧
                            P.g uGS = 0 ∧
                              Filter.Tendsto (P.uiter γ u0) Filter.atTop (nhds uGS) ∧
                                2 * P.β * GPDYS.mGS uGS ^ 2 ≤ P.nu uGS ∧
                                  0 < P.nu uGS ∧
                                    0 < GPDYS.η γ μ (P.nu uGS) ∧
                                      GPDYS.η γ μ (P.nu uGS) < 1 ∧
                                        ∃ K,
                                          ∀ (k : ℕ),
                                            K ≤ k →
                                              P.lyap γ (P.iter γ u0 (k + 1)) - P.energy uGS ≤
                                                  (1 - GPDYS.η γ μ (P.nu uGS)) *
                                                    (P.lyap γ (P.iter γ u0 k) - P.energy uGS) ∧
                                                P.energy (P.uiter γ u0 k) - P.energy uGS +
                                                      P.R γ (P.iter γ u0 k) ^ 2 / γ +
                                                    P.nu uGS / 4 * P.nrm2sq (P.uiter γ u0 k - uGS) ≤
                                                  2 * (1 - GPDYS.η γ μ (P.nu uGS)) ^ (k - K) *
                                                    (P.lyap γ (P.iter γ u0 K) - P.energy uGS)

μmax

def · Spectral.lean:49 · GPDYS.Setting.μmax
On a nonempty mesh, μ_max = max {⟪v, -Δ_h v⟫_h : v ∈ 𝓜} is the largest eigenvalue of -Δ_h. The empty-set value is zero.
def μmax : ℝ := sSup {x : ℝ | ∃ v : ι → ℝ, P.nrm2sq v = 1 ∧ x = P.kin v}

theorem · Spectral.lean:105 · GPDYS.Setting.μmax_isEigenvalue
μ_max is an eigenvalue of -Δ_h: -Δ_h v = μ_max v for a maximizer v ∈ 𝓜 of the Rayleigh quotient.
GPDYS.Setting.μmax_isEigenvalue :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι],
  ∃ v, P.nrm2sq v = 1 ∧ P.negLap v = P.μmax • v

eigenvalue_le_μmax

theorem · Spectral.lean:129 · GPDYS.Setting.eigenvalue_le_μmax
Every eigenvalue of -Δ_h is at most μ_max.
GPDYS.Setting.eigenvalue_le_μmax :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι] {v : ι → ℝ},
  v ≠ 0 → ∀ {l : ℝ}, P.negLap v = l • v → l ≤ P.μmax

rate_theorem_μmax

theorem · Spectral.lean:138 · GPDYS.Setting.rate_theorem_μmax
Theorem 5.1 with μ = μ_max, the largest eigenvalue of -Δ_h, exactly as in the paper: the iterates converge to the discrete ground state u_GS, ν ≥ 2β m_GS² > 0, the rate η = min{γν/(4(1+γμ_max)), 1/2} satisfies 0 < η < 1, and (5.6) holds from some index K on.
GPDYS.Setting.rate_theorem_μmax :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι],
  2 ≤ Fintype.card ι →
    ∀ {u0 : ι → ℝ},
      (∀ (i : ι), 0 < u0 i) →
        P.nrm2 u0 = 1 →
          ∀ {γ : ℝ},
            0 < γ →
              γ ≤ P.γmax u0 →
                ∃ uGS,
                  (∀ (i : ι), 0 < uGS i) ∧
                    P.nrm2 uGS = 1 ∧
                      P.g uGS = 0 ∧
                        Filter.Tendsto (P.uiter γ u0) Filter.atTop (nhds uGS) ∧
                          2 * P.β * GPDYS.mGS uGS ^ 2 ≤ P.nu uGS ∧
                            0 < P.nu uGS ∧
                              0 < GPDYS.η γ P.μmax (P.nu uGS) ∧
                                GPDYS.η γ P.μmax (P.nu uGS) < 1 ∧
                                  ∃ K,
                                    ∀ (k : ℕ),
                                      K ≤ k →
                                        P.lyap γ (P.iter γ u0 (k + 1)) - P.energy uGS ≤
                                            (1 - GPDYS.η γ P.μmax (P.nu uGS)) *
                                              (P.lyap γ (P.iter γ u0 k) - P.energy uGS) ∧
                                          P.energy (P.uiter γ u0 k) - P.energy uGS + P.R γ (P.iter γ u0 k) ^ 2 / γ +
                                              P.nu uGS / 4 * P.nrm2sq (P.uiter γ u0 k - uGS) ≤
                                            2 * (1 - GPDYS.η γ P.μmax (P.nu uGS)) ^ (k - K) *
                                              (P.lyap γ (P.iter γ u0 K) - P.energy uGS)

nu_attained

theorem · Spectral.lean:165 · GPDYS.Setting.nu_attained
ν is attained: it is the minimum of ⟪w, ℋ w⟫_h over the unit tangent vectors w ∈ 𝓜, ⟪p, w⟫_h = 0, as in the paper.
GPDYS.Setting.nu_attained :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι),
  2 ≤ Fintype.card ι →
    ∀ {p : ι → ℝ},
      P.nrm2sq p = 1 →
        (∀ (i : ι), 0 < p i) → P.g p = 0 → ∃ w, P.nrm2sq w = 1 ∧ P.inner p w = 0 ∧ P.hessForm p w = P.nu p

GPDYS/Sign.lean — Remark 2.3: uniqueness of the minimizer up to sign (irreducibility from the positive resolvent)

MS_mulVec_apply

theorem · Sign.lean:32 · GPDYS.Setting.MS_mulVec_apply
The i-th component of (M + γ S) x.
GPDYS.Setting.MS_mulVec_apply :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) (γ : ℝ) (x : ι → ℝ) (i : ι),
  (P.M + γ • P.S).mulVec x i = P.w i * x i + γ * ∑ j, P.S i j * x j

S_irreducible

theorem · Sign.lean:40 · GPDYS.Setting.S_irreducible
Irreducibility of S (connectivity of the mesh), derived from the entrywise positivity of the resolvent: for every proper nonempty subset A of the indices there are i ∈ A and j ∉ A with S i j ≠ 0.
GPDYS.Setting.S_irreducible :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) (A : Finset ι),
  A.Nonempty → Aᶜ.Nonempty → ∃ i ∈ A, ∃ j ∉ A, P.S i j ≠ 0

same_sign_of_kin_le

theorem · Sign.lean:90 · GPDYS.Setting.same_sign_of_kin_le
If v has no zero component and vᵀ S v ≤ |v|ᵀ S |v|, then all components of v have the same sign.
GPDYS.Setting.same_sign_of_kin_le :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {v : ι → ℝ},
  (∀ (i : ι), v i ≠ 0) → (P.kin v ≤ P.kin fun i => |v i|) → (∀ (i : ι), 0 < v i) ∨ ∀ (i : ι), v i < 0

eq_or_eq_neg_of_energy_le

theorem · Sign.lean:153 · GPDYS.Setting.eq_or_eq_neg_of_energy_le
Remark 2.3, uniqueness up to sign: if u > 0 is a critical point of E_h on 𝓜 and v ∈ 𝓜 satisfies E_h(v) ≤ E_h(u), then v = u or v = -u.
GPDYS.Setting.eq_or_eq_neg_of_energy_le :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) {u v : ι → ℝ},
  P.nrm2sq u = 1 → (∀ (i : ι), 0 < u i) → P.g u = 0 → P.nrm2sq v = 1 → P.energy v ≤ P.energy u → v = u ∨ v = -u

exists_groundState_sign

theorem · Sign.lean:189 · GPDYS.Setting.exists_groundState_sign
Remark 2.3 in full: there is a positive u_GS ∈ 𝓜 which is a critical point of E_h|_𝓜, the global minimizer of E_h on 𝓜, the unique nonnegative critical point, and the minimizer is unique up to sign: every minimizer of E_h on 𝓜 is u_GS or -u_GS.
GPDYS.Setting.exists_groundState_sign :
∀ {ι : Type u_1} [inst : Fintype ι] [inst_1 : DecidableEq ι] (P : GPDYS.Setting ι) [Nonempty ι],
  ∃ uGS,
    (∀ (i : ι), 0 < uGS i) ∧
      P.nrm2 uGS = 1 ∧
        P.g uGS = 0 ∧
          (∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.nrm2 v = 1 → P.energy uGS ≤ P.energy v) ∧
            (∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.nrm2 v = 1 → (∀ (i : ι), 0 ≤ v i) → P.g v = 0 → v = uGS) ∧
              ∀ (v : ι → ℝ), P.nrm2 v = 1 → P.energy v ≤ P.energy uGS → v = uGS ∨ v = -uGS
